# Formable Nations: Reworked - toggle sync
# Every toggle lives as a variable on every country. Countries that appear mid-game (released, formed,
# created by events) copy all of them from a country that already has them, so a formable switched
# off in the settings stays off for new countries too.

# Root = the new country. Copies every category and individual toggle from REFERENCE (a saved scope).
fnr_copy_formable_toggles_effect = {
	if = { limit = { scope:$REFERENCE$ = { has_variable = fnr_historical_enabled } } set_variable = { name = fnr_historical_enabled value = yes } }

# Root = a country without toggles. Finds a synced country and copies from it.
fnr_sync_formable_toggles_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = fnr_toggles_synced } }
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = fnr_toggles_synced
				NOT = { this = root }
			}
			save_scope_as = fnr_toggle_reference
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:fnr_toggle_reference }
			fnr_copy_formable_toggles_effect = { REFERENCE = fnr_toggle_reference }
		}
		else = {
			# Nobody is synced yet (a save from before this system): treat every country as synced
			every_country = {
				set_variable = fnr_toggles_synced
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------- Counting ----------
# How many formables the current settings allow (shown in the settings and Individual Formables windows)

fnr_count_fnr_formables_effect = {
	set_variable = { name = $VAR$ value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_variable = fnr_pamiristan_enabled
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_variable = fnr_pamiristan_disabled }
					has_variable = fnr_modern_enabled
				}
			}
		}
		change_variable = { name = $VAR$ add = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_variable = fnr_hashashin_enabled
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_variable = fnr_hashashin_disabled }
					has_variable = fnr_nonhistorical_enabled
				}
			}
		}
		change_variable = { name = $VAR$ add = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_variable = fnr_county_of_tripoli_enabled
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_variable = fnr_county_of_tripoli_disabled }
					has_variable = fnr_nonhistorical_enabled
				}
			}
		}
		change_variable = { name = $VAR$ add = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_variable = fnr_county_of_edessa_enabled
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_variable = fnr_county_of_edessa_disabled }
					has_variable = fnr_nonhistorical_enabled
				}
			}
		}
		change_variable = { name = $VAR$ add = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_variable = fnr_armenian_cilicia_enabled
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_variable = fnr_armenian_cilicia_disabled }
					has_variable = fnr_historical_enabled
				}
			}
		}
		change_variable = { name = $VAR$ add = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_variable = fnr_jorvik_enabled
				AND = {

fnr_count_vanilla_formables_effect = {
	set_variable = { name = $VAR$ value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_variable = fnr_vanilla_sca_enabled
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_sca_disabled }
					has_variable = fnr_historical_enabled
				}
			}
		}
		change_variable = { name = $VAR$ add = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_variable = fnr_vanilla_sweden_enabled
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_sweden_disabled }
					has_variable = fnr_historical_enabled
				}
			}
		}
		change_variable = { name = $VAR$ add = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_variable = fnr_vanilla_denmark_enabled
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_denmark_disabled }
					NOT = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_formables_disabled }
				}
			}
		}
		change_variable = { name = $VAR$ add = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_variable = fnr_vanilla_drd_enabled
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_drd_disabled }
					NOT = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_formables_disabled }
				}
			}
		}
		change_variable = { name = $VAR$ add = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_variable = fnr_vanilla_gbr_enabled
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_gbr_disabled }
					NOT = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_formables_disabled }
				}
			}
		}
		change_variable = { name = $VAR$ add = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_variable = fnr_vanilla_eng_enabled
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_eng_disabled }
					NOT = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_formables_disabled }
				}
			}
		}
		change_variable = { name = $VAR$ add = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_variable = fnr_vanilla_wls_enabled
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_wls_disabled }
					NOT = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_formables_disabled }
				}

# Per-category counts (fnr_active_cat_<category>), shown on the settings buttons
fnr_count_formables_by_category_effect = {
	set_variable = { name = fnr_active_cat_historical value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = fnr_active_cat_nonhistorical value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = fnr_active_cat_althistory value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = fnr_active_cat_modern value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = fnr_active_cat_controversial value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = fnr_active_cat_fantasy value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = fnr_active_cat_meme value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = fnr_active_cat_us_states value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = fnr_active_cat_vanilla value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_variable = fnr_pamiristan_enabled
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_variable = fnr_pamiristan_disabled }
					has_variable = fnr_modern_enabled
				}
			}
		}
		change_variable = { name = fnr_active_cat_modern add = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_variable = fnr_hashashin_enabled
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_variable = fnr_hashashin_disabled }

				}
			}
		}
